Motivation: 地理空间基础模型可提供卫星影像的可复用表征，支持低任务特定建模的下游制图。本文旨在评估年度AlphaEarth嵌入能否支持区域尺度的耕地-非耕地二分类制图，以探索低算力、免微调方案在区域农业制图中的可行性。

Method: 使用192个空间分离的影像块，标签来自USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL)。在不微调基础模型的情况下，采用轻量分类器（逻辑回归、梯度提升集成、最近类质心）进行二分类。开展样本效率实验（6万 vs 860万像素）与跨年份迁移实验（2018-2023）。并与CDL及微调后的TerraMind分割模型在385个随机点进行盲式双判读共识对比。

Result: 留出块上整体精度93.7%，平衡精度90.8%；逻辑回归与梯度提升集成相差0.3个百分点内，最近类质心达90.2%。6万平衡样本与860万全量像素相差1.3个百分点内。跨年迁移在各年份保持准确。与人工共识对比，AlphaEarth+随机森林一致率95.3%（κ=0.82），优于CDL的91.7%（κ=0.72，p=0.0161）；与微调TerraMind差异不显著（93.5%，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 冻结算子的地理空间嵌入可作为区域耕地制图的低算力候选方案，结果与CDL标签噪声的部分平滑一致，但未证明全州范围的参考产品纠正。研究受限于单州、30米衍生训练参考及单块人工验证，尚不能作为计算成本的受控比较。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models provide reusable representations of satellite imagery that support downstream mapping with limited task-specific modelling. We evaluate whether annual AlphaEarth embeddings support binary cultivated-versus-non-cultivated mapping in Maine, USA, using 192 spatially separated patches and labels derived from the USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Without fine-tuning the foundation model, a lightweight classifier reaches 93.7% overall accuracy and 90.8% balanced accuracy on held-out patches. Logistic regression is within 0.3 percentage points of a gradient-boosted ensemble, while a nearest-class-centroid rule, which uses class centroids but fits no parameters, reaches 90.2%. A balanced sample of 60,000 labelled pixels is within 1.3 percentage points of the full pool of 8.6 million pixels; because pixels are spatially autocorrelated, this result concerns pixel-sample efficiency rather than 60,000 independent annotation sites. In a same-region transfer experiment, classifiers trained in one year remain accurate across 2018 to 2023. Against a blind, two-interpreter consensus at 385 randomly sampled points in one contiguous 2023 block, the AlphaEarth-plus-random-forest map agrees at 95.3% ($κ=0.82$), compared with 91.7% for the CDL ($κ=0.72$; exact two-sided McNemar $p=0.0161$). This local result is consistent with partial smoothing of CDL label noise, but it does not establish statewide correction of the reference product. On the same points, the difference from a fine-tuned TerraMind segmentation model is not statistically significant (95.3% versus 93.5%; $p=0.14$), and the experiment is not a controlled comparison of computational cost. These results support frozen geospatial embeddings as a low-compute candidate for regional cropland mapping, subject to the limits of a single-state study, a 30 m-derived training reference, and a one-block human validation.

</details>


### [74] [ResLRP: The Role of Residual Cancellation in Attribution Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17152)
*Jim Berend,Reduan Achtibat,Daniel Schäffer,Alexander Binder,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Maximilian Dreyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ResLRP，通过在LRP传播规则中显式处理残差连接中的抵消效应，解决了ViT归因中的相关性爆炸问题，显著提升归因质量，尤其在VLM上提升巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT虽已成为主流视觉模型，但其输入归因难以同时做到细粒度、忠实和稳定。现有适配Transformer注意力的LRP在ViT上产生噪声大、不忠实的解释。作者发现关键缺失在于残差连接的处理：残差路径中的抵消效应导致归因爆炸，且这种抵消在ViT中比语言Transformer更严重。

Method: 提出Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP)，是LRP的简单扩展。其传播规则显式考虑残差分支中的抵消效应，具有精确守恒性，并能可证明地限制相关性爆炸。通过因果通道级干预验证残差抵消（而非一般正则化效应）是导致不稳定的原因。

Result: ResLRP在忠实性和定位性上大幅提升归因质量，评估覆盖监督、自监督、对比、层次化和多模态等ViT架构族，以及真值可控的FunnyBirds基准。在现代VLM上收益最大，定位提升+27–29%，忠实性分数最高提升3.4倍。此外，ResLRP还能在输入空间中定位SAE特征，其残差放大度量可作为预测归因退化位置的架构级诊断工具。

Conclusion: 残差连接中的抵消效应是ViT归因不稳定的核心原因。ResLRP通过显式建模残差抵消，提供保守且有界的传播，在多种视觉架构和基准上显著改善归因质量，并为VLM和SAE特征定位提供实用工具。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are central to most modern vision models, yet obtaining input attributions that are fine-grained, faithful, and stable remains challenging. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) has been adapted to transformer attention, but in ViTs it often produces noisy, unfaithful explanations. We show that the missing ingredient is the treatment of residual connections: cancellation effects in residual pathways lead to attribution explosion. Moreover, we find that these cancellations are substantially stronger in ViTs than in language transformers. To address this issue, we introduce Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP), a simple extension of LRP whose propagation rules explicitly account for cancellations in residual branches, are exactly conservative, and provably bound relevance explosion. Causal channel-wise interventions confirm that residual cancellation, not a generic regularization effect, drives the instability. ResLRP substantially improves attribution quality across faithfulness and localization, evaluated on ViT architectures spanning supervised, self-supervised, contrastive, hierarchical, and multimodal families, as well as on the ground-truth-controlled FunnyBirds benchmark. The largest gains arise in modern Vision Language Models (VLMs), with +27-29% localization and up to 3.4x faithfulness scores. Beyond benchmarks, ResLRP localizes Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) features in input space, and our residual amplification measure serves as an architecture-level diagnostic predicting where attribution degrades.

</details>


### [75] [MUMINS: Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17169)
*Anna Oliveras,Roger Marí,Rafael Redondo,Oriol Guardià,Cynthia Ifeyinwa Ugwu,Ana Tost,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Carolina Migliorelli,Vicent Ribas,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MUMINS，一种高效的元数据条件、不确定性感知的医学图像下一状态合成扩散框架，将基线扫描与其随访残差联合扩散并求和生成随访扫描，同时在单次反向扩散过程中预测空间不确定性图，在肺CT和脑MRI上匹配或超越领域专用SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测肿瘤生长、神经退行等解剖变化是困难的生成视觉任务：形态演化相对静态解剖极为细微、高度患者特异且本质随机。现有方法存在问题：确定性网络忽略生物随机性，标准扩散模型需多次采样才能量化不确定性，计算代价高昂。

Method: MUMINS将基线扫描与其随访残差联合扩散，求和以合成随访扫描，并在同一单次反向扩散过程中同时预测空间不确定性图。以时间间隔与相关元数据为条件，通过在每个去噪步骤动态重新注入基线作为软锚点来保留细粒度解剖结构；用负对数似然头学习不确定性图以显式标记易错区域。不含器官特定启发式，通过各数据集单独重训练即可跨解剖结构复用同一架构。

Result: 大量评估表明，MUMINS经数据集特定重训练后，在肺CT（PNG）和脑MRI（OASIS-3）上匹配或优于专用、领域特定的最先进方法。

Conclusion: MUMINS以单次反向扩散实现随访图像合成与不确定性预测的统一，架构通用、无需器官特定设计，重训练后可跨解剖结构达到或超越领域专用SOTA方法。

Abstract: Forecasting anatomical changes such as tumor growth and neurodegeneration is a challenging generative vision task. Morphological evolution is subtle relative to static anatomy, highly patient-specific, and inherently stochastic. Existing methods struggle with several issues: deterministic networks ignore biological stochasticity, while standard diffusion models require computationally prohibitive multi-pass sampling to quantify uncertainty. We propose MUMINS (Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis), an efficient diffusion framework that jointly diffuses a baseline scan and its follow-up residual, summed to synthesize the follow-up scan, while concurrently predicting a spatial uncertainty map, in a single reverse diffusion process. Conditioned on the time interval and relevant metadata, it preserves fine-grained anatomy by dynamically re-injecting the baseline as a soft anchor at every denoising step, and a negative-log-likelihood head learns the uncertainty map to explicitly flag error-prone regions. Designed without organ-specific heuristics, the same architecture is reused across anatomies via separate, dataset-specific retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that dataset-specific retraining of MUMINS matches or outperforms dedicated, domain-specific state-of-the-art methods on lung CT (PNG) and brain MRI (OASIS-3). Project page: https://github.com/aolivtous/MUMINS.

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Cultural Heritage Architectural Style Classification for Residential Buildings in the UAE Based on CLIP Embeddings and SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17181)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Iman Ibrahim,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态机器学习框架，利用CLIP模型融合图像视觉特征与专家文本描述，对阿联酋住宅建筑风格进行分析与分类，取得98%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN的建筑风格分类过度依赖视觉图像，且缺乏针对非西方地区的特定数据集；本文旨在填补阿联酋住宅建筑风格分析这一空白。

Method: 采用OpenAI的CLIP模型，将图像视觉特征与专家文本描述融合为统一的512维嵌入；使用UMAP进行降维可视化，并用K-Means做无监督聚类；基于人工分析聚类结果得到的标签训练SVM分类器，实现自动化建筑风格分类。

Result: 在识别出的八个风格聚类上实现98%的分类准确率，高于文献中其他所有研究，验证了融合视觉与文本模态的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了多模态AI在建筑遗产分析中的潜力，为探索区域性建筑特征提供了可扩展且可解释的工具。

Abstract: The analysis and classification of cultural heritage architectural styles remain challenging due to the complexity of visual images of buildings, which are highly relied on in traditional CNN-based classification approaches in comparison to textual descriptions, and the relative lack of non-western region-specific datasets. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a multimodal machine learning framework to analyze and classify Emirati residential architecture using OpenAI's CLIP model. We integrate visual features from images and textual features from expert descriptions into a unified 512-dimensional embedding, followed by dimensionality reduction with UMAP for visualization and unsupervised clustering using K-Means. Cluster labels, which are derived from manual analysis of the K-Means clusters, are used to train an SVM classifier for automated architectural style classification. Our approach achieves a classification accuracy of 98% across eight identified style clusters, higher than every other study in the literature, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining visual and textual modalities. Overall, this paper highlights the potential of using multimodal AI to support architectural heritage analysis, offering scalable and interpretable tools for exploring regional architectural identities.

</details>


### [77] [EventEgoHands++: Event-based Egocentric 3D Hand Mesh Reconstruction with Real Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17189)
*Ryosei Hara,Wataru Ikeda,Masashi Hatano,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EventEgoHands++，一个从第一视角进行基于事件相机的3D手部网格重建框架，通过手部检测器估计左右手的实例级边界框与掩码，并引入自适应注意力机制动态门控手间交互建模，同时构建了大规模真实数据集 EEH-R，在合成与真实数据上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于传统相机的手部3D重建在低光照和严重运动模糊下表现不佳。事件相机虽具高动态范围和高时间分辨率优势，但在第一视角（egocentric）场景中，佩戴者自身运动产生大量背景事件，掩盖了手部信号。现有首个第一视角事件方法虽用掩码缓解该问题，但其二值掩码无法区分左右手，导致缺乏实例级信息、在单手或无手时仍预测双手，进而造成手间关系错误和重建精度下降。

Method: 提出 EventEgoHands++ 框架：1）引入手部检测器（Hand Detector），为左手和右手分别估计实例级边界框与掩码；2）提出自适应注意力（Adaptive Attention），基于检测结果动态门控注意力，以准确学习双手之间的空间关系与相互交互。

Result: 扩展了合成数据集 N-HOT3D，并新建了 EEH-R——迄今最大的真实事件相机第一视角手部数据集，包含约100万标注帧，覆盖低光照等环境。在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入实例级手部检测与自适应注意力机制，EventEgoHands++ 有效解决了第一视角事件相机手部重建中左右手区分与手间交互建模问题，并借助新构建的大规模真实数据集验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: 3D hand mesh reconstruction is a challenging yet essential task for downstream applications, including human-robot interaction and AR/VR. Although conventional cameras have been widely adopted for this task, methods that rely on them struggle in low-light environments and under severe motion blur. To address these limitations, event-based cameras have recently attracted attention for their high dynamic range and high temporal resolution. However, applying event cameras to egocentric hand reconstruction remains challenging because camera wearer's motion produces dense background events that obscure hand-specific signals. Although the first egocentric event-based approach mitigates this issue using hand segmentation, its binary hand mask does not distinguish between left and right hands. As a result, the model lacks instance-level hand information and predicts both hands even when only one or neither hand is present. This limitation leads to incorrect inter-hand relationships and degraded reconstruction accuracy. In this paper, we propose EventEgoHands++, a framework for event-based 3D hand mesh reconstruction from an egocentric viewpoint. The proposed method incorporates a Hand Detector that estimates instance-level bounding boxes and masks for both the left and right hands. Moreover, we introduce Adaptive Attention, which dynamically gates the attention based on these detection results to accurately learn the spatial relationship and mutual interactions between the hands. To train and evaluate our framework, we extend the synthetic N-HOT3D dataset and newly construct EEH-R, the largest real-world event-based egocentric hand dataset to date, comprising approximately 1M annotated frames captured in environments including low-light conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Probe-VAD: Ordinal Likelihood Probing for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17211)
*Jiawei Gu,Qilin Zhao,Tengkuo Guo,Zhiming Zhong,Shuangqing Zhang,Fan Lyu,Fang Zhao,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Probe-VAD，一种序数二分类探测框架，通过直接探测冻结 VLM 的严重程度偏好来实现免训练视频异常检测，避免标题压缩和预定义分数接口的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的免训练视频异常检测在视觉理解与异常打分之间设置了受限接口：基于标题的流程把视觉证据压缩成文本，可能丢失细微线索；直接数值生成则迫使模型用少量预定义分数表达判断。这些接口会掩盖异常严重程度的细微差异，使视觉上不同的片段获得相似表示或分数，限制异常排序的分辨率。

Method: Probe-VAD 直接探测冻结 VLM 的严重程度偏好。给定原始视频片段，查询十个有序的严重程度阈值，提取受约束的 YES/NO 延续似然度。其归一化偏好构成累积严重程度轮廓，从中聚合尾部证据形成连续异常分数，并用保序投影（isotonic projection）强制序数一致性。

Result: 在公开视频异常检测基准上，Probe-VAD 以较低计算成本取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: Probe-VAD 提供了一个简单接口，将冻结 VLM 的视觉理解转化为连续、对排序敏感的异常分数，无需任务特定训练，也无需基于标题的压缩。代码已开源。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to localize anomalous events in untrimmed videos. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide rich visual understanding for training-free VAD, but existing approaches impose restrictive interfaces between visual understanding and anomaly scoring. Caption-based pipelines compress visual evidence into text, potentially discarding subtle cues, while direct numerical generation forces the model to express its judgment through a small set of predefined scores. Such interfaces can obscure subtle differences in anomaly severity, causing visually distinct clips to receive similar representations or scores and thereby limiting the resolution of anomaly ranking. We propose \textbf{Probe-VAD}, an ordinal binary-probing framework that directly probes severity preferences from a frozen VLM. Given raw video clips, Probe-VAD queries ten ordered severity thresholds and extracts constrained \textit{YES}/\textit{NO} continuation likelihoods. Their normalized preferences form a cumulative severity profile, from which tail evidence is aggregated into a continuous anomaly score, with isotonic projection enforcing ordinal consistency. Experiments on public VAD benchmarks demonstrate superior performance with low computational cost. Probe-VAD provides a simple interface for translating frozen VLM visual understanding into continuous, rank-sensitive anomaly scores without task-specific training or caption-based compression. Code is available at: https://github.com/yvestine/COVAS-VAD.

</details>


### [79] [InfoTaxa: Information-Calibrated Label-Free Clustering for Fine-Grained Visual Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17218)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用信息校准的聚类分析研究冻结视觉嵌入的无标签聚类，发现仅靠图像信息存在粗到细的失败模式，物种级聚类同时受聚类方法与表征信息量双重限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签聚类为生物多样性监测提供可扩展途径，但仅用图像的细粒度分类存在稳定的"粗到细"失败模式：聚类能恢复粗略分类结构，却在物种级停滞。作者希望诊断这一瓶颈究竟是方法限制还是信息限制。

Method: 在BIOSCAN-5M上进行信息校准的聚类分析。使用BioCLIP~2特征配合UMAP与HDBSCAN，并与oracle-K、图方法和学习式聚类头对比。提出InfoTaxa，将聚类效率（无监督划分所恢复的探针估计图像信息比例）与配对DNA作为审计信号（非推理输入）结合，并进行留出晚期融合探针、多编码器与稀有类鲁棒性分析。

Result: BioCLIP~2+UMAP+HDBSCAN在科级达0.79 AMI、属级0.67 AMI，优于先前图像基线，与oracle-K、图方法及学习头在相同冻结特征上相当。密度管道在目级和科级分别恢复约0.90和0.81的图像可用信息。留出晚期融合探针显示，在图像嵌入中加入DNA可将物种级预测误差降低约两比特。

Conclusion: 在被测试的设定中，物种级无标签聚类既受聚类限制也受表征限制：改进聚类可恢复更多图像暴露的结构，但无法单独弥合经DNA审计的信息缺口。

Abstract: Label-free clustering of frozen pretrained visual embeddings offers a scalable route to biodiversity monitoring, but image-only fine-grained taxonomy exhibits a consistent coarse-to-fine failure mode: clusters recover broad taxonomic structure yet plateau at species level. We study this behaviour on BIOSCAN-5M through an information-calibrated clustering analysis. BioCLIP~2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN reach $0.79$ AMI at family and $0.67$ at genus, substantially improving over the prior image baseline and remaining competitive with oracle-$K$, graph-based, and learned clustering heads on the same frozen features. To diagnose whether the remaining plateau is method-limited or information-limited, we introduce InfoTaxa, which combines clustering efficiency---the fraction of probe-estimated image information recovered by an unsupervised partition---with paired DNA as an audit signal only, not an inference input. The density pipeline recovers approximately $0.90$ and $0.81$ of the image-available information at order and family, respectively. Held-out late-fusion probes show that adding DNA to the image embedding reduces species-level prediction error by approximately two bits. Robustness analyses cover multiple image encoders, described-species and rare-class subsets, probe diagnostics, and held-out-species coarse-rank generalisation and same-species retrieval. Thus, in the tested setting, species-level label-free clustering is both clustering-limited and representation-limited: improved clustering may recover additional image-exposed structure, but cannot close the DNA-audited information gap alone.

</details>


### [80] [FROD: Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17227)
*Yanbin Hou,Biao Xiong,Guojun Xu,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将甲骨文辅助破译建模为跨时代图像翻译任务，提出FROD框架，通过特征匹配门控分割监督、残差去噪扩散模型与多阶段字体风格化精修，在字符不相交数据集上取得优于基线的Top-1识别准确率，较OBSD绝对提升3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲骨文是早期汉字书写系统，对汉语词源研究意义重大。传统破译高度依赖领域专家，需要分析语义语境与结构演变，过程劳动密集，因此需要自动化辅助手段。

Method: 作者将甲骨文破译辅助形式化为跨时代图像翻译任务，提出FROD。方法包括三部分：(1) 利用快速特征匹配提供门控分割监督，高相似度配对按patch处理以对齐细粒度部首，低相似度配对整体训练以避免错配伪影；(2) 采用残差去噪扩散模型(RDDM)联合估计噪声与残差信号，缓解位置漂移与笔画混乱；(3) 多阶段字体风格化精修网络去除边缘噪声并稳定笔画结构。

Result: 在增强的字符不相交数据集上，FROD的Top-1识别准确率高于所评估基线，相比OBSD取得3.8%的绝对增益。

Conclusion: FROD通过结合特征匹配门控监督、残差去噪扩散与风格化精修，有效提升了甲骨文到现代汉字的跨时代图像翻译与识别性能，为甲骨文破译提供自动化辅助。

Abstract: Oracle bone script (OBS), one of the earliest Chinese writing systems, plays an important role in the study of Chinese etymology. Traditional decipherment relies heavily on domain experts who analyze characters through semantic context and structural evolution. To assist this labor-intensive process, we formulate OBS decipherment assistance as a cross-era image translation task and propose FROD (Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher). Although many OBS characters differ substantially from their modern counterparts, they often preserve local topological invariants at the radical level. During training, FROD leverages fast feature matching to provide gated segmentation supervision: paired samples with sufficient matches are processed patch-wise to align fine-grained radicals, whereas low-similarity pairs are trained holistically to avoid mismatched artifacts. In addition, a Residual Denoising Diffusion Model (RDDM) jointly estimates noise and residual signals, thereby reducing the positional drift and stroke disorder commonly observed in standard diffusion models. Finally, a multi-stage font stylization refinement network refines the generated images by eliminating edge noise and stabilizing stroke structures. On our augmented character-disjoint dataset, FROD achieves higher Top-1 recognition accuracy than the evaluated baselines, with a 3.8% absolute gain over OBSD.

</details>


### [81] [DecoGS: Adaptive Static-Dynamic Decoupling of 3D Gaussians for Free-Viewpoint Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17230)
*Idil Sulo,Alexey Supikov,Ilke Demir,Sainan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoGS提出一种高效在线训练3D高斯的方法，通过自适应选择性地优化动态或光度变化区域，在流式视频重建中实现快速、高保真且时序稳定的场景更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建需要在速度与时序保真度之间取得平衡。现有方法每帧都更新所有高斯，即使在静态区域也进行冗余更新，导致闪烁和漂移。因此需要一种只针对真正变化区域进行优化的机制。

Method: DecoGS引入自适应机制，将优化聚焦于存在运动或光度变化的时空区域；结合区域感知的高斯管理，包括梯度门控（gradient gating）和高效可见性过滤（visibility filtering），以保持时序一致性和紧凑的内存占用。

Result: 在N3DV和MeetRoom数据集上分别达到34.55 dB和31.60 dB PSNR，优于所有流式和离线基线；渲染速度261 FPS，时序闪烁比最佳先前方法低70倍，且无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: 通过避免静态区域的冗余更新并采用区域感知的高斯管理，DecoGS在流式3D高斯重建中有效兼顾了速度、质量和时序稳定性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction demands both speed and temporal fidelity, goals that existing methods undermine by updating every Gaussian every frame, even in static regions. We present DecoGS, a method for efficient online training of 3D Gaussians from streaming videos. Unlike prior methods that update the entire scene indiscriminately, DecoGS introduces an adaptive mechanism that selectively focuses optimization on spatiotemporal regions exhibiting motion or photometric changes. This targeted training strategy eliminates redundant updates that cause flickering and drift in nominally static regions, while enabling fast, high-fidelity scene updates. The pipeline further integrates region-aware Gaussian management through gradient gating and efficient visibility filtering to maintain temporal coherence and a compact memory footprint. On N3DV and MeetRoom, DecoGS achieves 34.55 and 31.60 dB PSNR respectively, outperforming all streaming and offline baselines, while rendering at 261 FPS with $70\times$ lower temporal flicker than the best prior method, requiring no large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [82] [Video-HolmesV2: Can MLLMs Reason with Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Evidence in Long Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17248)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Zipeng Wang,Yushe Cao,Chenhui Qiang,Shuaibing Cheng,Xuesong Yang,Sen Nie,Bowen Jiang,Wenchao Ding,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Xuehui Yu,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准与音频-文本引导的 token 压缩框架，面向深度音视频耦合的视频叙事推理，强化基于证据的评测，并缓解长上下文噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型的视频理解评测偏重视觉、忽视听觉线索，使模型成为"沉默的观察者"，回避真正的跨模态推理；同时标准密集采样带来证据-上下文权衡：增加帧数虽可捕捉证据，却导致注意力分散与 token 爆炸。

Method: 提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准，强调基于证据的评测，要求模型以精确时空音视频证据佐证答案；引入多模型交叉验证流程保障任务严谨性，以及时空证据感知指标进行细粒度校准；并提出音频-文本引导的 token 压缩框架，融合任务意图与听觉锚点，蒸馏高价值推理线索以缓解长上下文噪声。

Result: 在评测中，即使强专有模型准确率也低于 60%，而所提方法优于同类开源全模态模型。

Conclusion: 该工作通过新基准与压缩框架，推动视频叙事推理从视觉主导转向深度音视频耦合，并提升长上下文处理效率与评测可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated impressive video understanding, yet their ability to reason over long-form narratives is often masked by visual-centric evaluations and inefficient context processing. Existing benchmarks over-rely on visual heuristics while marginalizing auditory cues, effectively reducing models to "silent observers" that bypass genuine cross-modal reasoning. Moreover, standard dense sampling creates an evidence-context trade-off: increasing frames to capture evidence inevitably leads to attention distraction and token explosion. To bridge these gaps, we present Video-HolmesV2, a novel benchmark designed for Deep Audio-Visual Coupling. Unlike previous works, it enforces an Evidence-Based Evaluation, requiring models to justify answers with precise spatio-temporal audio-visual evidence, thereby reducing confounding effects of guessing and hallucinated evidence. To support this, we introduce: (1) a Multi-Model Cross-Verification pipeline to ensure task rigor; (2) a Spatio-temporal Evidence-Aware Metric for fine-grained calibration. Furthermore, we propose an Audio-Text Guided Token Compression framework. By fusing task intent with auditory anchors, our method distills high-value reasoning cues to mitigate long-context noise. In our evaluation, even strong proprietary models achieve below 60% accuracy, while our approach outperforms comparable open-source omni-models.

</details>


### [83] [Exploring 2D backbone effects for indoor semantic occupancy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17257)
*Shizhang Fanga,Wanling Yea,Qi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在EmbodiedScan的RGB-D语义占据预测中，仅替换2D图像骨干（CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2、DINOv2），固定其余流程，mIoU从17.41%到30.55%大幅变化，DINOv2最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RGB-D具身语义占据预测流程中，图像编码器常被视为默认模块，但其特征正是后续被采样到3D体素的视觉证据。作者希望直接研究这一设计选择，探究2D骨干对最终3D占据预测的影响究竟有多大。

Method: 保持主要RGB-D投影、深度分支和占据头不变，仅替换图像骨干，在受控条件下对比CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2和DINOv2四种编码器，并报告mIoU及类别级结果。

Result: mIoU变化显著：DINOv2为30.55%，BLIP2为29.49%，CLIP-ViT为24.33%，CLIP-ResNet为17.41%。更强编码器无需修改下游3D融合流程即可超过原始EmbodiedScan ResNet-50基线。类别上DINOv2在布局与结构类更强，BLIP2在部分物体类接近；CLIP-ViT明显优于CLIP-ResNet。

Conclusion: 图像骨干并非具身语义占据中的次要工程细节，而是最终3D预测变异的主要来源；同时CLIP特征作为密集token的暴露方式对体素提升也很重要。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy prediction gives an embodied agent a voxel-level account of where space is free, occupied, and semantically meaningful. In RGB-D pipelines such as EmbodiedScan, the image encoder is often left as a default module, even though its features are the visual evidence later sampled into the 3D grid. We study this design choice directly. A central finding is that changing the 2D backbone improves occupancy accuracy more than several carefully designed occupancy architectures or modules. We keep the main RGB-D projection, depth branch, and occupancy head fixed, and replace only the image backbone. The compared encoders are CLIP-ResNet, CLIP-ViT, BLIP2, and DINOv2. Under the controlled setting, the measured mIoU changes substantially: DINOv2 obtains 30.55\%, BLIP2 obtains 29.49\%, CLIP-ViT obtains 24.33\%, and CLIP-ResNet obtains 17.41\%. The stronger encoders also exceed the original EmbodiedScan ResNet-50 baseline without modifying the downstream 3D fusion pipeline. Class-level results give a more detailed picture: DINOv2 is stronger on many layout and structural categories, whereas BLIP2 remains close on several object-centered classes. CLIP-ViT improves clearly over CLIP-ResNet, showing that the way CLIP features are exposed as dense tokens matters for voxel lifting. These results indicate that the image backbone is not a secondary engineering detail in embodied semantic occupancy, but a major source of variation in the final 3D prediction.

</details>


### [84] [Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification for Mitigating Object Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17269)
*Ziheng Ren,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法SSAV，通过跨查询语义一致性与区域定位一致性验证多模态大模型的对象声明，有效缓解物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型能根据视觉输入生成自然语言回复，但可能提及图像中不存在的物体。在用药辅助、无障碍感知和环境决策等场景中，此类幻觉会带来现实安全风险，因此需要一种可靠的对象声明验证方法。

Method: SSAV（语义-空间一致性验证）是免训练方法。其假设：有视觉依据的声明应在语义等价查询下保持稳定，并反复定位到同一图像区域。方法通过聚合多个提示估计语义支持度，降低对查询措辞的敏感性；查询诱导区域验证（QIRV）结合跨查询区域持续性、空间重叠和相对候选优势，识别孤立高响应与分散定位；最后用几何平均融合语义与空间证据，任一支路缺乏支持时降低验证分。

Result: 在三个基座模型和多种评估协议上，SSAV有效缓解物体幻觉。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，COCO、A-OKVQA和GQA的平均准确率在POPE Popular和Adversarial下分别提升1.81和3.17个百分点，CHAIRs从49.40%降至32.80%。

Conclusion: 跨查询语义稳定性与区域一致性可为对象声明提供可解释的外部视觉证据。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models generate natural-language responses from visual inputs, yet may mention objects absent from an image. In medication assistance, accessible perception, and environmental decision-making, such hallucinations can create real-world safety risks. We propose Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification (SSAV), a training-free method for verifying object claims. A visually grounded claim should remain stable across semantically equivalent queries and repeatedly localize to the same image region. SSAV aggregates multiple prompts to estimate semantic support and reduce sensitivity to query wording. Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) combines cross-query region persistence, spatial overlap, and relative candidate dominance to identify isolated high responses and dispersed localizations. A geometric mean fuses semantic and spatial evidence, lowering the verification score when either branch lacks support. Experiments on three base models and multiple evaluation protocols show that SSAV effectively mitigates object hallucination. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, accuracy averaged across COCO, A-OKVQA, and GQA improves by 1.81 and 3.17 percentage points under POPE Popular and Adversarial, respectively, while CHAIRs decreases from 49.40% to 32.80%. These results show that cross-query semantic stability and regional consistency provide interpretable external visual evidence for object claims.

</details>


### [85] [Optical-Flow Wingbeat Counting in MuJoCo: A Comparison of Convolutional, Spiking, and Attention-Based Temporal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17308)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在MuJoCo中对虚拟Crazyflie相机观测的扑翼飞行器进行拍翅计数评估，比较卷积、脉冲与自注意力三类时间模型配合统一越阈事件计数器，1.5 m与3.0 m距离下精确计数准确率均较高且差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器的视觉监测需要从运动强度和平均频率中区分出单次拍翅，而现有方法在因果、实时的光学流条件下缺乏受控评估，因此需要检验从有符号光流中计数拍翅的可行性及其误差边界。

Method: 在MuJoCo中搭建受控实验：Crazyflie虚拟相机观测三种扑翼模型，光学距离为1.5与3.0 m，共240组配对场景生成1,440个片段，按场景3:1划分训练测试。采用共享空间卷积编码器，分别接因果时间卷积网络、循环泄漏积分发放脉冲网络或因果自注意力；各模型预测相位与活动度，再经相同的定向越阈事件计数器输出计数。保留六个已有卷积模型，十二个新模型冻结后生成测试预测。

Result: 1.5 m处精确计数准确率分别为96.67%、95.00%、96.67%；3.0 m处分别为94.44%、92.22%、95.00%。所有配对场景自助法区间均包含零。七个远距离脉冲模型片段总数正确但事件时间不匹配，说明总计数与事件级指标需同时报告。

Conclusion: 结果支持在测试条件下进行因果光流拍翅计数的可行性，并揭示了边界敏感误差；但不足以确立架构排序，也无法证明重复训练、真实飞行鲁棒性或硬件效率方面的优势。

Abstract: Visual monitoring of flapping-wing vehicles requires distinguishing individual wingbeats from motion strength and average frequency. This paper presents a controlled MuJoCo evaluation of wingbeat counting from signed optical flow observed by virtual cameras mounted on Crazyflie vehicles. Three flapping-wing models were recorded at optical distances of 1.5 and 3.0 m, producing 1,440 clips from 240 paired scene configurations with a scene-level 3:1 training-test split. A common spatial convolutional encoder was combined with a causal temporal convolutional network, a recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire spiking network, or causal self-attention. Each model predicted phase and activity, followed by the same directed-crossing event counter. The six existing convolutional models were retained, and all twelve new models were frozen before their test predictions were generated. Exact-count accuracies at 1.5 m were 96.67%, 95.00%, and 96.67%, respectively; at 3.0 m they were 94.44%, 92.22%, and 95.00%. All paired scene-bootstrap intervals for differences in exact-count accuracy included zero. Seven far-distance spiking-model clips had correct totals despite event-timing mismatches, demonstrating why total-count and event-level measurements must be reported together. The results support the feasibility of causal optical-flow counting in the tested setting and identify boundary-sensitive errors. They do not establish an architecture ranking across repeated training, real-flight robustness, or hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [PanoGS-SLAM: Panoramic 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17387)
*Yongqi Mao,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Xiangfei Guo,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PanoGS-SLAM，首个基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的全景稠密 SLAM 系统，直接在球面域进行可微渲染与位姿优化，实现全向光度约束，并通过球面一致光度损失与深度引导高斯初始化提升几何一致性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时稠密 SLAM 是机器人在动态或快速变化环境中实现鲁棒定位与高质量建图的核心能力。现有基于 3DGS 的 SLAM 方法大多面向窄视场针孔相机，角度覆盖受限削弱了位姿可观测性，在快速运动和大幅视角变化下常导致光度优化不稳定，因此需要全景方案以增强可观测性与稳定性。

Method: 在球面域直接进行可微渲染与位姿优化，提供全向光度约束以实现更稳定的跟踪；引入（1）球面一致光度损失，补偿等距柱状投影的面积畸变；（2）深度引导的高斯初始化策略，稳定新观测区域的增量建图。

Result: 在真实与合成全景基准（PALVIO 和 SynPano）上，PanoGS-SLAM 在跟踪精度与渲染质量上持续优于几何基线和基于 GS 的基线，同时实现快速前端收敛和实时性能。受控视场实验显示，随角度覆盖增加，优化条件和收敛稳定性呈明显单调改善。

Conclusion: 全景传感几何显著改善基于可微高斯的 SLAM 优化景观；PanoGS-SLAM 验证了全景稠密 SLAM 的可行性与优势，源代码将公开。

Abstract: Real-time dense SLAM is a core capability for robotics applications that require robust localization and high- quality mapping in dynamic or fast-changing environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based SLAM methods have shown promising performance, but most are designed for narrow-FoV pinhole cameras, where limited angular coverage weakens pose observability and often leads to unstable photo- metric optimization under rapid motion and large viewpoint changes. We present PanoGS-SLAM, the first panoramic dense SLAM system built on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method per- forms differentiable rendering and pose optimization directly in the spherical domain, enabling omnidirectional photometric constraints for more stable tracking. To improve geometric consistency and robustness, we introduce (1) a sphere-consistent photometric loss that compensates for the area distortion of equirectangular projection, and (2) a depth-guided Gaussian initialization strategy that stabilizes incremental mapping in newly observed regions. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic panoramic benchmarks (PALVIO and SynPano) show that PanoGS-SLAM consistently outperforms geometric and GS-based baselines in tracking accuracy and rendering quality, while achieving fast front-end convergence and real-time perfor- mance. In addition, controlled field-of-view experiments reveal a clear monotonic improvement in optimization conditioning and convergence stability as angular coverage increases, high- lighting the fundamental role of sensing geometry in shaping the optimization landscape of differentiable Gaussian-based SLAM. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [SSC-Priors: Exploring Semantic and Visibility Priors to Boost Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17413)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过给现有激光雷达语义场景补全网络输入语义伪标签和可见性信息，无需重构网络即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年SSC方法多追求架构创新，模型日益复杂沉重，本文回归简单策略，探索以语义伪标签和传感器可见性作为额外输入来提升现有方法。

Method: 将离线段分割器生成的语义伪标签和区分空白空间与未知空间的可见性信息，作为额外输入直接提供给SSC网络，仅需对原架构做最小改动，并研究可见性信息的数据设计空间。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI上，四种架构的老模型经增强后可与最先进系统竞争，其中一个甚至超越；在SSCBench-nuScenes上，两种先验在32线稀疏传感器下也能迁移。

Conclusion: 高质量语义先验是语义增益（mIoU）的主要驱动因素，模型仅需用真值语义训练一次即可适配任意分割器；可见性信息带来次要提升，简单先验可有效增强现有SSC方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates easy strategies to boost the performance of existing networks for lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. The fact is that, over the last years, SSC methods have mostly pursued architectural innovations, making the models heavier and more complex, e.g., by jointly training a point cloud semantic segmentation branch. In this work, we take a step back and explore two priors used as simple ingredients (possibly noisy) to improve existing approaches: semantic pseudo-labels and sensor visibility information. Concretely, we provide both kinds of information directly as additional inputs to a given SSC network, requiring only a minimal adaptation of the original architecture. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmenters significantly improves the performance of existing SSC models. In fact, by evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of semantic gains (mIoU), and that the SSC model can be trained just once with ground-truth semantics and then exploited without retraining using any segmenter. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar point cloud with visibility information that distinguishes between empty spaces (between the lidar and a scanned point) and unknown spaces (outside of lines of sight), providing a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. We study the design space of data for representing visibility information and bound the remaining headroom with a ground-truth oracle on the free-space labels. On SemanticKITTI, these enhancements make older models competitive with state-of-the-art systems across four architectures, in one case even outperforming them. On the SSCBench-nuScenes benchmark, both priors also transfer with the sparser 32-beam sensor.

</details>


### [88] [SlotDiT: Object-Centric Representations for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17414)
*Gjergj Plepi,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SlotDiT，一个在基于 slot 的物体中心潜在空间上运行的文本引导扩散 Transformer，用于机器人视频生成与规划；实验表明 slot 表示兼顾生成质量与任务完成率，且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件潜在扩散模型依赖像素级或 VAE 潜在表示，缺乏显式语义结构，表示空间的影响尚未被充分探索；而 slot 类物体中心表示虽在动态建模与规划中有效，却未被用于扩散生成建模。

Method: SlotDiT 是文本引导的扩散 Transformer（DiT），在 slot 潜在空间操作：给定参考图像与语言指令，将场景分解为表示各实体的物体中心 slot，并在指令与场景上下文条件下自回归地去噪未来 slot 轨迹以预测场景动态。同时在统一 DiT 框架内对比 slot、VAE 与语义对齐三类潜在表示。

Result: 以 slot 作为 DiT 潜在表示可取得有竞争力的视频生成质量，并在四个机器人数据集上持续提升任务完成率；其紧凑表示相比 VAE 与语义对齐潜在空间更具计算效率。

Conclusion: 物体中心结构是机器人环境中扩散生成建模的一种强有力的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Text-conditioned latent diffusion models perform strongly in video generation and are promising backbones for robotic applications. However, existing approaches rely on pixel-level or VAE-based latent representations that lack explicit semantic structure, leaving the impact of the representation space largely unexplored. Slot-based object-centric representations offer a structured alternative by decomposing scenes into object-level latents, or slots. While they have shown success in dynamics modeling and planning, they have not yet been explored for diffusion-based generative modeling. We introduce SlotDiT, a text-guided Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in a slot-based latent space. Given a reference image and a language instruction, SlotDiT decomposes the scene into object-centric slots representing individual entities. Conditioned on the instruction and observed scene context, the model autoregressively denoises future slot trajectories to predict scene dynamics. To systematically investigate latent-space design for diffusion transformers, we compare slot-based representations against VAE-based and semantics-aligned alternatives within a unified DiT framework. Our experiments show that using slots as DiT latents yields competitive video generation quality while consistently improving task-completion rates across four robotic datasets. Furthermore, their compact representation provides a computationally efficient alternative to VAE-based and semantics-aligned latent spaces. Overall, our results demonstrate that object-centric structure is a powerful inductive bias for diffusion-based generative modeling in robotic environments. The project page is available at https://slot-dit.github.io/.

</details>


### [89] [Tracking the Unseen: An Occlusion-Robust Framework for Target Tracking Under Full and Long-Term Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17427)
*Mais Mohammed,Sharifa Mohammed,Hanan Awadh,Haneen Bamaas,Raghad Bawazeer,Elham Alghamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种抗遮挡目标跟踪框架，融合YOLOv11n检测、卡尔曼滤波运动预测与遮挡感知外观重识别，在OVIS及自建数据集上显著优于OccluTrack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多目标跟踪系统在完全及长期遮挡下十分脆弱，目标短暂或完全消失会导致轨迹提前终止、身份丢失，进而削弱国防与监控场景的态势感知能力。

Method: 框架分三阶段：目标检测、遮挡期间位置估计、目标重现后身份恢复；整合YOLOv11n检测、卡尔曼滤波运动预测与遮挡感知外观重识别（OAMN）。在相同跟踪框架与条件下评估了六种Re-ID架构，OAMN综合表现最佳并被选用。

Result: 在公开OVIS数据集上较OccluTrack，MOTA相对提升18.1%、IDF1相对提升25.1%、身份切换减少12.8%；在模拟长期遮挡的自建数据集上，MOTA为0.734、IDF1为0.729，较OccluTrack相对提升14.2%与5.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架在挑战性遮挡条件下展现出良好的跟踪连续性、身份保持与轨迹估计能力，适用于需在目标可见性丢失期间持续跟踪的国防相关监控应用。

Abstract: Real-time multi-object tracking systems remain highly vulnerable to full and long-term occlusion, where targets temporarily or completely disappear from the camera's field of view. Conventional trackers may terminate trajectories prematurely, resulting in identity loss and reduced situational awareness in applications such as defense and surveillance. This work proposes an occlusion-robust target tracking framework that maintains target identity and trajectory continuity through the integration of YOLOv11n object detection, Kalman Filter motion prediction, and occlusion-aware appearance-based re-identification. The framework consists of three stages: object detection, position estimation during occlusion, and identity recovery after target reappearance. Six Re-Identification (Re-ID) architectures were evaluated within the same tracking framework under identical conditions, with the Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) achieving the best overall performance and therefore selected for the final pipeline. The framework was benchmarked against OccluTrack on the public OVIS dataset, achieving relative improvements of 18.1 percent in Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA) and 25.1 percent in Identity F1 Score (IDF1), while reducing identity switches by 12.8 percent. On a custom military dataset simulating surveillance and battlefield-like environments with long-term occlusion, the framework achieved a MOTA of 0.734 and an IDF1 of 0.729, corresponding to relative improvements of 14.2 percent and 5.8 percent over OccluTrack. The system demonstrated strong tracking continuity, robust identity preservation, and reliable trajectory estimation under challenging occlusion conditions, highlighting its effectiveness for defense-related surveillance applications requiring continuous target tracking during visibility loss.

</details>


### [90] [BrainFocus: EEG-Guided ROI Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17443)
*Yihui Peng,Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainFocus 提出一种脑电（EEG）引导的高效视觉问答框架：用 EEG 分类器预测目标类别，YOLO 检测器定位对应 ROI，仅当两者都通过置信度阈值时才将裁剪区域送入 VLM，否则处理整图，从而在杂乱场景中提升准确率并大幅降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在 VQA 上表现强劲，但处理大幅杂乱图像时计算开销高，而实际相关区域往往很小。EEG 能反映人对视觉刺激的神经响应，可提供关于感兴趣区域（ROI）的人类语义线索，但 EEG 引导的视觉类别解码并不完美，直接做 ROI 路由不可靠。

Method: 提出 BrainFocus 框架：EEG 分类器预测目标类别，YOLO 检测器定位匹配的 ROI；仅当两个预测都通过置信度阈值时，VLM 才只接收裁剪后的 ROI，否则处理完整图像，以此实现可靠的 EEG 引导高效推理。评估方面基于 EEG-ImageNet 构建了 40 类基准，包含生成的杂乱图像与真实物体中心图像，并配有目标 ROI 标注和 600 个英文视觉问答对。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-VL 2B、4B 和 9B 模型上，BrainFocus 在杂乱场景中将 VQA 准确率提升 4.14–9.87 个百分点，同时将输入 token 和总 token 减少 23.2%–39.4% 与 23.2%–39.3%，端到端浮点运算量（FLOPs）减少 23.2%–39.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使 EEG 的语义解码并不完美，EEG 仍可有效引导 VLM 的高效推理，在提升准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual question answering (VQA) performance, but processing large cluttered images is computationally expensive when only a small region is relevant. Electroencephalography (EEG) signals, which capture human neural responses to visual stimuli, can provide a human-derived semantic cue about the region of interest (ROI). However, EEG-guided visual category decoding remains imperfect, making direct ROI routing unreliable. In this work, we propose BrainFocus, a reliable EEG-guided efficient VLM framework for VQA. An EEG classifier predicts a target category, and a YOLO detector localizes the matching ROI. The VLM receives the cropped ROI only when both predictions pass confidence thresholds; otherwise, it processes the full image. For evaluation, we build on EEG-ImageNet to construct a 40-class benchmark comprising generated cluttered images and real object-centric images, with target-ROI annotations and 600 English visual question-answer pairs. Across Qwen3.5-VL 2B, 4B, and 9B models, BrainFocus improves VQA accuracy by 4.14-9.87 percentage points (pp) on cluttered scenes while reducing input tokens and total tokens by 23.2%-39.4% and 23.2%-39.3%, and end-to-end floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 23.2%-39.5%. These results demonstrate that EEG can guide efficient VLM inference even when its semantic decoding is imperfect.

</details>


### [91] [ORCA: Occlusion-Aware Refinement and Completion for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17450)
*Weronika Jakubowska,Maciej Zięba,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA是一种遮挡感知的单图三维场景重建与补全方法，通过优先复用重建中已有的RGB-D信息修复小的遮挡缺失，仅对大区域使用生成式补全，从而减少幻觉并提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像新视角合成本质上是病态问题：相机偏离原视角后，原本被遮挡的区域暴露出来，导致几何缺失和空洞。现有方法多依赖生成模型补全这些区域，但许多伪影其实只是深度边界附近的小缝隙，并不需要生成新的场景内容，而生成过程昂贵且易引入幻觉。

Method: ORCA首先利用单目深度将三维结构引入高斯锚点（Gaussian-anchor）表示，同时保持原始相机光线对应关系。场景探索时按缺失区域的大小与结构分类处理：小范围遮挡缺失用重建中已有的RGB-D信息修复；仅当区域较大且无法从场景可靠恢复时才保留生成式修补。新增的高斯锚点在局部进行优化，不改动已有表示。

Result: 在DIV2K数据集上，ORCA在所有报告指标上均优于VistaDream，MUSIQ由61.60提升至68.71，CLIP-IQA由0.474提升至0.574。

Conclusion: 结果表明，许多新视角合成伪影可以通过复用重建场景中已有的信息被有效修复，从而减少对生成式补全的不必要依赖，抑制生成导致的幻觉，并更好地保持原始场景的内容与结构。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis from a single image is a fundamentally ambiguous problem. As the camera moves away from the input viewpoint, previously hidden regions become visible, exposing missing geometry and holes in the reconstructed scene. Existing methods often rely on generative models to complete such regions. However, many of these artifacts are small gaps near depth boundaries and do not require generating new scene content.
  In order to eliminate expensive process of generating image we introduce ORCA, an occlusion-aware method for reconstructing and completing explorable 3D scenes from a single image. ORCA first introduces 3D structure into a Gaussian-anchor representation using monocular depth while preserving the original camera-ray correspondence. During scene exploration, missing regions are handled based on their size and structure. Small disocclusions are repaired using RGB-D information already available in the reconstruction, while generative inpainting is reserved for larger regions that cannot be reliably recovered from the scene. New Gaussian anchors are added and optimized locally without modifying the existing representation. By reducing unnecessary reliance on generative inpainting, ORCA limits generation-induced hallucinations and better preserves the content and structure of the original scene.
  On DIV2K, ORCA improves novel-view quality over VistaDream across all reported metrics, increasing MUSIQ from 61.60 to 68.71 and CLIP-IQA from 0.474 to 0.574. These results show that many novel-view artifacts can be repaired effectively by reusing information already present in the reconstructed scene.

</details>


### [92] [Tables Decoded: DELTA for Structure, TARQA for Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17458)
*Jahanvi Rajput,Dhruv Kudale,Saikiran Kasturi,Utkarsh Verma,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DELTA与TARQA，用结构化文本表示（OTSL）替代表格图像输入，实现表格重建与表格视觉问答，并在多语言场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格理解方法多依赖视觉语言模型处理表格图像，需语言特定的视觉编码器，处理难度大且不利于多语言文档，因此作者探索更易处理、更契合LLM的结构化文本表示。

Method: DELTA将物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别与OCR分离，提取准确的布局与内容，并输出为紧凑统一的OTSL格式；在此基础上微调LLM得到TARQA，用于OTSL序列上的表格问答。

Result: 在FinTabNet、PubTabNet、PubTables-1M上，DELTA的TEDS-Structure与SOTA相当；在自建印地语基准TORQUE上验证鲁棒性；TARQA在WTQ上提升9.3个百分点，在FinTabNetQA上提升9.2个百分点；TORQUE上排名第二。

Conclusion: 结构化文本表示是表格理解的可扩展且有效的替代方案，尤其适合多语言文档，相关代码、模型与基准已开源。

Abstract: Table understanding is a core task in document intelligence, encompassing two key subtasks: table reconstruction and table visual question answering (TabVQA). While recent approaches predominantly rely on vision- language models (VLMs) operating on table images, we propose a more scalable and effective alternative based on structured textual representations. These representations are easier to process, align more naturally with LLMs, and eliminate the need for language-specific visual encoders, making them particularly suitable for multilingual documents. We present DELTA, which separates physical structure recognition, logical structure recognition, and OCR to extract both layout and content accurately. DELTA outputs tables in Optimised Table Structure Language (OTSL), a compact and unified format that encodes cell arrangements and textual content. On table structure recognition (TSR), DELTA achieves TEDS- Structure scores comparable with state-of-the-art methods across FinTabNet, PubTabNet, and PubTables-1M. We further establish its robustness on non-English tables through our curated Hindi benchmark, TORQUE. Building on this, we introduce TARQA, an LLM fine-tuned on OTSL sequences. Our approach yields gains of 9.3 p.p. on WTQ (TabQA) and 9.2 p.p. on FinTabNetQA (TabVQA), respectively. On TORQUE, our method ranks second among all VLMs and DELTA + LLM variants. We release our code, models, and benchmark at: https://github.com/Tihiitborg/Tables-Decoded

</details>


### [93] [Det-LIME: Detector-Aware, Multi-Instance Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations for Automated Marine Mammal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17479)
*Jiayi Zhou,David W. Johnston,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出检测器感知的多实例解释方法 Det-LIME，为海洋哺乳动物（以及其他群体性生物）目标检测提供实例级、框对齐的高分辨率解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱目标检测器在海洋哺乳动物研究中应用迅速，但可解释性技术很少融入保护工作流；现有面向分类的解释工具不适合涉及社会性/群居生物影像的检测任务，因为它们忽略场景中的多个检测目标，输出单一实例结果并把不同个体的证据混在一起，且生成的视觉解释分辨率低、常缺乏生物学意义，难以用于调试、定向数据增强和精细化数据采集。

Method: Det-LIME：对 LIME 做检测器感知、多实例适配，通过逐检测加权、强调每个检测框附近区域的邻近核，以及基于 IoU 的匹配来在扰动间追踪同一实例，从而生成实例特异、与检测框对齐的解释。在港口海豹检测的无人机航拍影像上评估，并增加海鸟案例研究检验通用性，与 vanilla LIME、Stabilized LIME、Deterministic LIME 及基于梯度的归因方法对比。

Result: 使用 Attribution Ratio 与 Max Saliency Hit Rate 指标，Det-LIME 在多实例归因上持续优于对比方法，并提供更高分辨率、实例感知的解释。

Conclusion: Det-LIME 的实例级解释有助于理解模型输出，支持后处理、调试，并可推动建模、数据采集与数据增强方面的可操作改进。

Abstract: Despite the rapid uptake of black-box object detectors in marine mammal research and monitoring, explainability techniques are rarely integrated into conservation workflows. Furthermore, most classification-oriented explainability tools are ill-suited to detection tasks involving imagery of social organisms or those with colonial life histories, as they ignore multiple detections within a scene and produce single-instance outputs that blur evidence across individuals. These methods also generate low-resolution, often biologically irrelevant visuals, limiting their utility for debugging, targeted data augmentation, and refined data collection.
  We proposed Det-LIME, a detector-aware, multi-instance adaptation of Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) that produced instance-specific, box-aligned explanations by combining per-detection weighting, a proximity kernel that emphasizes regions near each box, and Intersection-over-Union-based matching to track the same instance across perturbations. We evaluated Det-LIME on aerial drone imagery for harbor seal detection, with an additional seabird case study to assess generality, and compared it with vanilla LIME, Stabilized LIME, Deterministic LIME, and gradient-based attribution methods.
  Using the Attribution Ratio and Max Saliency Hit Rate metrics, we showed that Det-LIME consistently improved multi-instance attribution. In practice, these higher-resolution, instance-aware explanations provide insight into model outputs and support post-processing, debugging, and actionable improvements in modeling and data collection or augmentation.

</details>


### [94] [PhysStream: Streaming Physics-Grounded Video Generation with Structured Scene Memory and Fine-Grained Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17521)
*Chuhao Chen,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang,Chen Wang,Qiao Feng,Sergey Tulyakov,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysStream，一种自回归图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化场景记忆与稀疏速度增量信号实现物理落地的细粒度交互式运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法需在生成前提供完整控制序列，或使用像素级信号仅指定物体位置而非物理动力学，无法支持生成过程中的交互控制。

Method: 引入结构化场景记忆（位置图与物体跟踪图，由已生成帧在线推导），并采用编码物理量的稀疏速度增量信号进行运动控制；两阶段训练：先对双向模型微调加入运动控制条件，再训练带结构化场景记忆的因果自回归模型。

Result: 在合成基准上，运动分布距离（FVMD）降低 33%，轨迹误差降低 12%；在真实场景对比中，超过 85% 的情况下被人类评估者偏好。

Conclusion: PhysStream 支持多物体桌面刚体场景的生成中交互控制，为先前方法所不具备，显著提升物理一致性与人类偏好。

Abstract: Interactive control for video generation is moving from coarse prompts toward fine-grained, physically meaningful manipulation of dynamic scenes. Yet existing controllable methods either require the full control schedule before generation starts, or use pixel-space signals that dictate object positions rather than physical dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose PhysStream, an autoregressive model for physics-grounded image-to-video synthesis that incorporates structured scene memory---positional maps and object tracking maps derived online from previously generated frames---and supports fine-grained motion control via sparse velocity-increment signals that encode physical quantities, letting the model learn the underlying dynamics. We train our model in two stages: a bidirectional model is first finetuned with motion-control conditioning, then a causal autoregressive model is trained with additional structured scene memory, further improving physical consistency. PhysStream enables interactive, mid-generation control over multi-object tabletop rigid-body scenes---a capability not supported by prior methods---reducing motion distribution distance (FVMD) by 33% and trajectory error by 12% over the strongest baselines on synthetic benchmarks, and is preferred by human evaluators in over 85% of in-the-wild comparisons. Please check our website for more details: https://czzzzh.github.io/PhysStream

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Causal neural set filtering for online multi-target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16054)
*Zhongdi Liu,Huangyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果神经集合滤波（CNSF），仅编码当前量测、以递归轨迹状态携带历史证据，通过排他Sinkhorn关联、关联条件卡尔曼式更新与矩匹配、以及循环伯努利生命周期建模，在多目标跟踪上相比Track-MT3显著降低GOSPA与T-GOSPA并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类多目标跟踪方法（如MT3/Track-MT3）将数据关联与状态估计联合学习，但会反复重新编码量测窗口，导致大量冗余计算，效率与实时性受限。

Method: 提出CNSF神经集合滤波器：只编码当前量测，用结构化递归轨迹状态承载历史证据；结合排他Sinkhorn关联、关联条件卡尔曼式更新与矩匹配、循环伯努利生命周期建模（含量测驱动的新生）。这些机制施加软一对一约束、传播关联引起的状态不确定性，并支持漏检与出生—死亡转变下的存在性估计。

Result: 在留出的三区间仿真测试集上，CNSF相对Track-MT3将平均GOSPA和T-GOSPA分别降低19.3%和30.4%，参数量减少55.9%，单线程CPU推理速度提升3.76倍。

Conclusion: CNSF以递归因果集合滤波替代重复窗口编码，在保持跟踪精度的同时大幅降低参数量与计算开销，验证了高效多目标跟踪的可行性。

Abstract: Transformer-based multi-target tracking (MTT) jointly learns data association and state estimation, but MT3/Track-MT3-style trackers repeatedly re-encode measurement windows, incurring redundant computation. We propose Causal Neural Set Filtering (CNSF)\footnote{\href{https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}{Code: https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}}, a neural set filter that encodes only current measurements while carrying past evidence in a structured recursive track state. CNSF combines exclusive Sinkhorn association, association-conditioned Kalman-shaped updates with moment matching, and recurrent Bernoulli lifecycle modeling with measurement-driven birth. These mechanisms impose soft one-to-one constraints, propagate association-induced state uncertainty, and support existence estimation under missed detections and birth--death transitions. On a held-out three-regime simulated test set, CNSF reduces mean GOSPA and T-GOSPA relative to Track-MT3 by 19.3\% and 30.4\%, with 55.9\% fewer parameters and a $3.76\times$ speedup in single-thread CPU inference.

</details>


### [96] [Managing Action Preconditions in Neuro-Symbolic RL: Three Placement Strategies for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16056)
*Norbert Oswald,Fabian Deuser,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将行为知识形式化为结构动作上的前置条件贝叶斯网络，并在强化学习循环的三个位置注入该符号知识（符号验证器、符号执行器、符号学习器），在MiniGrid和Fetch两个基准上验证了三种注入方式对求解质量、样本效率和可追溯性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会将熟悉情境下的行为知识带入新任务而非从头学习，RL智能体也应如此。神经符号RL虽能结合先验知识与学习策略，但知识注入的位置至关重要：选择不当会产生幻觉式前置条件，导致智能体在变化环境中出现安全与可靠性问题。

Method: 将行为知识形式化为智能体结构动作（如拾钥匙、抓方块、开关门、放物体等合法性依赖前置条件的动作）上的前置条件贝叶斯网络，用以限制这些动作的触发时机，并在RL循环中用三种位置注入：（1）符号验证器，仅在推理时查询，前置条件满足时触发结构动作；（2）符号执行器，在训练与推理全程管控结构动作的使用；（3）符号学习器，将知识融入网络，自行学习结构动作的限制与使用。

Result: 在具有相反特性的两个基准（长序列规划链的MiniGrid与连续操作的Fetch）上实验。MiniGrid上三种注入方式均提升了求解质量，其中符号执行器以98.2%领先于PPO+RND基线的88.8%；Fetch上的结果在摘要中未完整给出。

Conclusion: 结构化的符号行为知识注入位置对RL智能体的表现影响显著，恰当的注入方式能在求解质量、样本效率和可追溯性上带来可观收益，神经符号RL为结合先验知识与学习策略提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Humans carry behaviour knowledge of how to act in familiar situations into every new task rather than relearning it from scratch. There is no reason a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent shouldn't do the same: known behaviour patterns need not be learned, only applied. Neuro-symbolic RL bridges prior knowledge and RL by injecting symbolic knowledge alongside a learned policy. The point at which this knowledge is integrated is critical: a poor choice can produce, for instance, hallucinated preconditions, which surface as safety and reliability problems in agents acting in changing environments. We formalise this behavioural knowledge as a precondition Bayesian network (BN) over the agent's \emph{structural actions} - the actions whose legality depends on preconditions, such as picking up a key, grasping a block, toggling a door, or dropping an object. The BN restricts when these actions may fire, and we inject it into the RL loop at three placements: (1) a \emph{symbolic verifier}, consulted only at inference, that fires a structural action once its preconditions hold; (2) a \emph{symbolic enforcer}, active during both training and inference, that governs structural-action use throughout learning; and (3) a \emph{symbolic learner}, which folds the knowledge into the network and learns the restriction and use of structural actions itself. To test the three variants we run experiments on two benchmarks with opposite regimes: one built on long, ordered planning chains, the other on continuous manipulation. We compare against strong baselines on solution quality, sample efficiency, and traceability. The payoff is substantial. On MiniGrid, all three placements improve the \emph{solution quality} over the PPO+RND baseline, the symbolic enforcer leading at $98.2\%$ against the baseline's $88.8\%$. On Fetch, $\dots$

</details>


### [97] [OmniHarness: Harnessing Generalizable Visual Generation via Symbolic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16057)
*Xu Xu,Jinxiu Liu,Zhangbo Qiao,Jiaxing Lu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yubin Gu,Fangwei Ning,Yan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniHarness 框架，通过符号策略学习实现可泛化的视觉生成，解决现有方法经验泛化差、反思滞后、知识被动获取三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型与多智能体视觉生成方法存在三大局限：(1) 任务经验蒸馏泛化性有限；(2) 反思往往延迟到任务完成后才进行；(3) 知识仅在下游任务需求出现时才被动获取。

Method: 将已验证的执行抽象为面向视觉生成任务族的符号策略，提取共享流程与适用条件并去除实例特定输入；该框架实例化、适配并组合策略以应对新任务，借助中间验证引导执行中的细化与失败恢复，并通过自主探究在下游目标明确前主动生成并执行接近能力边界的练习任务，利用执行反馈持续优化策略而模型参数保持固定。

Result: 在六个基准、三个 MLLM 主干和三个视觉智能体框架上的实验显示性能强劲且能力持续扩展；在 ComfyBench 的 Creative 任务上达到 95.0% 的解决率，超出最强基线 27.5 个百分点；冻结的策略快照可通过即插即用方式改进现有视觉智能体系统。

Conclusion: OmniHarness 通过符号策略学习实现了可泛化、可持续扩展的视觉生成能力，并可即插即用地增强现有视觉智能体系统。

Abstract: Unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and multi-agent systems have advanced visual generation. However, three limitations remain. (1) Existing methods often distill task-specific experience with limited generalizability. (2) Reflection is often deferred until task completion. (3) Knowledge is often acquired only in response to downstream task demands. To address these limitations, we introduce OmniHarness, a framework for generalizable visual generation via symbolic policy learning. OmniHarness abstracts verified executions into symbolic policies for visual generation task families, capturing shared procedures and applicability conditions while removing instance-specific inputs. The harness instantiates, adapts, and composes these policies for new tasks. Intermediate verification guides refinement and failure recovery during execution. Through self-directed inquiry, OmniHarness autonomously generates and executes practice tasks near its capability limits before downstream objectives are specified. Execution feedback continually refines the policies while model parameters remain fixed. Experiments across six benchmarks, three MLLM backbones, and three visual agent frameworks demonstrate strong performance and continual capability expansion. On ComfyBench's Creative tasks, OmniHarness achieves a 95.0% resolve rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5 percentage points. Frozen policy snapshots improve existing visual agent systems through plug-and-play reuse.

</details>


### [98] [Driver Behavior Estimation at Signalized Intersections Using a Physics-Constrained Decision-Conditioned Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16058)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,Pavel Laskov,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过真实车辆数据研究信号灯转换时驾驶员的停走决策与纵向轨迹行为，提出两阶段建模框架，实现高精度轨迹预测与舒适度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红灯违规和信号交叉口的急刹车是交通事故的主要原因，需要理解和预测驾驶员在信号灯转换时的决策与纵向轨迹行为。

Method: 采集449次接近、不同速度和距离下的真实驾驶数据，用RTK-GNSS记录车辆运动、监测心率和舒适度评分；统计发现所需减速度是停走决策的主导预测因子，并对峰值减速度进行异方差高斯建模得到五个舒适区间；提出两阶段框架：阶段1预测二元决策，阶段2用带物理约束（目标状态条件、加加速度限制）的决策条件自回归Transformer生成纵向加速度轨迹。

Result: 该架构优于基线方法，达到0.49m/s²的加速度MAE和0.62m的距离MAE；可从单个黄灯亮起快照估计驾驶员未来停车舒适度；定性结果显示类人的真实制动行为。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效预测信号灯转换下的驾驶决策与轨迹，数据集和源代码已公开。

Abstract: Red-light violations and harsh braking at signalized intersections are major contributors to traffic accidents. This paper analyzes and predicts human driver decision-making and longitudinal trajectory behavior during traffic light signal transitions. We collected a diverse real-world dataset comprising 449 approach runs under varying speed and distance conditions. Vehicle motion was recorded using RTK-corrected GNSS with centimeter-level accuracy, and driver heart rate and multi-level comfort ratings were monitored. Spatial and temporal calibration ensured precise alignment between vehicle state and signal timing. Statistical analysis identifies required deceleration as the dominant single predictor of the stop-go decision, and heteroscedastic Gaussian modeling of peak deceleration reveals five empirical comfort ranges derived from human stopping behavior. Based on this insight, we propose a two-stage modeling framework. Stage 1 predicts the binary maneuver decision, and Stage 2 generates the longitudinal acceleration trajectory using a decision-conditioned autoregressive Transformer with physics constraints, including target-state conditioning and jerk limits. The proposed architecture outperforms baseline methods and achieves 0.49m/s^2 acceleration MAE and 0.62m distance MAE. It also estimates the future stopping-comfort level of the human driver from a single yellow-onset snapshot. Qualitative results demonstrate realistic human-like braking behavior. The dataset and source code are publicly available.

</details>


### [99] [HintMiner: Automatic Question Hints Mining From Q&A Web Posts with Language Model via Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16060)
*Zhenyu Zhang,JiuDong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HintMiner，一种自动为在线问答用户挖掘问题提示的工具，利用机器阅读理解和序列生成技术，从海量网络问答帖子中提取提示，基于Transformer和复制机制构建编码器-解码器MiningNet，并以自监督目标训练，在6万个Stack Overflow问题上平均BLEU达36.17%、ROUGE-2达36.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在Stack Overflow等问答论坛上提问时，往往无法及时获得恰当的回答，因此需要一种自动化的方式帮助用户更快找到答案，即自动为问题生成有用的提示。

Method: HintMiner首先检索大量网络问答帖子，然后使用MiningNet从帖子中提取提示。MiningNet是一个基于Transformer和复制机制的神经编码器-解码器模型，借助海量在线问答帖子设计自监督目标进行训练，结合了机器阅读理解与序列生成技术。

Result: 在60,000个Stack Overflow问题上进行评估，实验结果表明方法有效：平均BLEU分数为36.17%，平均ROUGE-2分数为36.29%。工具和实验数据已公开。

Conclusion: HintMiner能够有效自动为用户问题生成提示，帮助用户更便捷地获取答案，且具备公开可复现的资源。

Abstract: Users often need ask questions and seek answers online. The Question - Answering (QA) forums such as Stack Overflow cannot always respond to the questions timely and properly. In this paper, we propose HintMiner, a novel automatic question hints mining tool for users to help them find answers. HintMiner leverages the machine comprehension and sequence generation techniques to automatically generate hints for users' questions. It firstly retrieve many web Q\&A posts and then extract some hints from the posts using MiningNet that is built via a language model. Using the huge amount of online Q\&A posts, we design a self-supervised objective to train the MiningNet that is a neural encoder-decoder model based on the transformer and copying mechanisms. We have evaluated HintMiner on 60,000 Stack Overflow questions. The experiment results show that the proposed approach is effective. For example, HintMiner achieves an average BLEU score of 36.17\% and an average ROUGE-2 score of 36.29\%. Our tool and experimental data are publicly available.

</details>


### [100] [POSPAN: Position-Constrained Span Masking for Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16061)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Lei Shen,Yuming Zhao,Meng Chen,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出POSPAN框架，通过组合span长度分布与位置约束分布，统一了现有span级掩码方法，实验证明位置约束能有效增强span级掩码，最佳设置优于单token MLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有span级掩码语言模型仅考虑span长度（离散分布），忽略了span之间的依赖关系，即假设掩码span位置均匀分布，未能充分利用实体/短语及其依赖对语言理解的价值。

Method: 提出POSPAN通用框架，通过span长度分布与位置约束分布的组合实现多样化的位置约束span掩码策略，将现有span级掩码方法统一起来；并在多个NLU基准数据集上进行预训练评估，同时对位置约束进行理论分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，位置约束能广泛提升span级掩码效果，最佳POSPAN设置持续优于仅考虑span长度的对应方法及原始MLM。

Conclusion: 位置约束在掩码语言模型中具有合理性，POSPAN框架具有必要性，理论分析进一步揭示了其有效性原因。

Abstract: Span-level masked language modeling (MLM) has shown to be advantageous to pre-trained language models over the original single-token MLM, as entities/phrases and their dependencies are critical to language understanding. Previous works only consider span length with some discrete distributions, while the dependencies among spans are ignored, i.e., assuming that the positions of masked spans are uniformly distributed. In this paper, we present POSPAN, a general framework to allow diverse position-constrained span masking strategies via the combination of span length distribution and position constraint distribution, which unifies all existing span-level masking methods. To verify the effectiveness of POSPAN in pre-training, we evaluate it on the datasets from several NLU benchmarks. Experimental results indicate that the position constraint is capable of enhancing span-level masking broadly, and our best POSPAN setting consistently outperforms its span-length-only counterparts and vanilla MLM. We also conduct theoretical analysis for the position constraint in masked language models to shed light on the reason why POSPAN works well, demonstrating the rationality and necessity of POSPAN.

</details>


### [101] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带符号、加权的仿射 p-adic 残差目标函数，将其作为有限域约束的原生编码方式，并用数独作为 81 系数案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望找到一种无需 one-hot 提升（one-hot lift）的原生编码方法，直接用 p-adic 残差来表达有限域约束，从而更紧凑地处理类数独等约束满足问题。

Method: 通过选择能区分有限字母表的素数，并使用充分加权的正一元行将系数限制在允许集合内，负行则奖励不等端点或子句满足；提出逐坐标支配定理，将全局极小值限制在有限域内，并分析损失函数与 all-different 冲突计数或 CNF 满足数之间的关系。

Result: 逐坐标支配定理保证每个全局极小值位于有限域内；在该域上，损失函数至多相差一个加性常数，即 all-different 冲突数或满足 CNF 子句数的负值。标准数独提供了 81 系数案例研究，且无需 one-hot 提升；客户端实现提供了生成的数据框、算术、诊断和搜索功能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明仿射 p-adic 残差目标可作为有限域约束的原生、紧凑编码，数独案例验证了其可行性，并提供配套实现工具。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [102] [You Don't Need To Train: Agentic Heuristic Learning Studio for Executable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16065)
*Siyu Yuan,He Zhang,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio工具，用类人认知学习（记忆样例、形成规则、修复错误）替代梯度反向传播进行人类活动识别（HAR），支持从数据集观察、启发式策略生成到无LLM边缘部署的端到端流程，并在11个HAR数据集上取得强性能且策略可检视、可编辑、可重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HAR依赖梯度训练的神经网络，本文希望探索更贴近人类认知的互补范式——人类通过记忆样例、形成规则、修正错误来学习活动，而非反向传播，从而获得可解释、可编辑、可部署的策略。

Method: 提出AHL Studio：学习型智能体在传感器协议上推理，生成可执行的启发式策略，记录修复轨迹，并导出无需LLM的边缘部署策略；面向HAR基准数据集族，提供从数据观察至边缘导出的一体化工作流。

Result: 在迄今评估的11个HAR数据集上，AHL策略达到较强的可执行策略性能，同时保持可检视、可编辑与可重放的特性。

Conclusion: AHL为HAR提供了区别于梯度训练的可解释启发式学习路径，兼具性能与边缘部署可用性，展示了以智能体驱动规则学习与修复的可行工作流。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is usually framed as gradient-based training of neural networks. Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio explores a complementary view inspired by human cognitive learning: people learn activities by remembering examples, forming rules, and repairing mistakes, not by backpropagating. This proposed tool implements AHL for HAR: a learning-time agent reasons over sensor protocols, proposes executable heuristic policies, records repair traces, and exports an LLM-free policy for edge deployment. We focus on the HAR benchmark family and provide an end-to-end workflow from dataset observation to edge-oriented export. On eleven HAR datasets evaluated so far, AHL policies reach strong executable-policy performance while remaining inspectable, editable, and replayable \footnote{https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio}.

</details>


### [103] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出全景性能预测框架，先预测Navier-Stokes方程基本物理量，再预测叶轮机性能参数，并引入Transformer增强神经算子(TNO)，精度优于FNO和DeepONet，下游任务计算成本降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶轮机设计中需要灵活快速的气动性能评估。多数已有预测模型直接预测目标函数，泛化性差，无法像CFD模拟器那样灵活应对多种设计目标。

Method: 提出全景性能预测框架：先预测Navier-Stokes方程基本参数（温度、压力、密度），再基于这些物理量预测涡轮级子午面关键性能参数。框架中引入Transformer增强神经算子(TNO)，以Rotor 37叶片为参考，训练预测跨音速压气机叶片子午面性能。

Result: TNO能准确预测等熵效率、质量流量等总量参数及总压比分布。预测误差小于FNO、DeepONet等先进深度学习算子。应用于敏感性分析和多目标优化等下游任务，计算成本较CFD降低四个数量级。

Conclusion: TNO可几乎像CFD模拟器一样工作，有效可靠地解决多种下游任务，验证了全景性能预测框架的实用价值。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane. By adopting this methodology, our proposed panoramic performance prediction framework functions similarly to a CFD simulator, capable of predicting various objective of interest to the designers. To enhance prediction accuracy, a transformer-enhanced neural operator (TNO) is introduced within this framework. Using the Rotor 37 blades as a reference, the proposed TNO is trained to predict the performance of a transonic compressor blade in the meridian plane. The TNO can accurately predict total quantities such as isentropic efficiency, mass flow, and distributions of total pressure ratio. Remarkably, the prediction error of TNO is observed to be smaller than that of state-of-the-art deep learning operators such as the FNO and DeepONet. Furthermore, the TNO is applied to downstream tasks, including sensitivity analysis and optimization of various objective functions. The results confirm that the TNO can operate almost like a CFD simulator, while reducing the computational cost of downstream tasks by four orders of magnitude. The effectiveness and reliability of the proposed TNO for solving different kinds of downstream tasks have been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [104] [A Dynamic Aggregation Strategy Enhanced Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm for Solving High-Dimensional Turbomachinery Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16067)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Yun Chen,Guangjian Ma,Liming Song,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种带动态聚合策略的高效全局优化算法DA-EGO，用于高维昂贵黑箱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维（d≥30）昂贵黑箱问题在有限评估预算下求解困难，需高效全局优化方法。

Method: 将高维空间分解为低维子空间进行代理模型优化，子空间最优解组合为精英点用于全局搜索；子空间不固定，每轮迭代依据子空间与全空间的变量交互分析更新变量，采用扰动法与方差分析检测交互；并据上一轮子空间优化结果自适应调整搜索范围。

Result: 在21个基准算例（7个函数，30/60/90维，1500次评估预算）上，DA-EGO对可分离与部分可分离问题有效；优势依情形而定，非可分离shifted Rosenbrock函数上GSGA在60、90维更优，30维与IKAEA、GSGA统计相当；在28变量跨声速转子叶片气动优化及60变量压气机级优化中亦表现出优势。

Conclusion: DA-EGO的有效性得到较充分验证。

Abstract: In order to solve the high-dimensional ($d \geq 30$) expensive black-box problems within budget, an efficient global optimization (EGO) algorithm with a dynamic aggregation strategy is proposed, labeled as DA-EGO. Specifically, the DA-EGO decomposes the original high-dimensional design space into a set of low-dimensional subspaces for efficient surrogate-based optimization search, and the optimal solutions of subspaces are combined as an elite point for the global search. Most importantly, the subspaces are not fixed. Instead, the subspace variables are updated in each iteration, according to the variable interaction analyses in the sub- and full-spaces. The perturbation method and the analysis of variance are used to detect variable interactions. To further accelerate the optimization progress, the searching ranges of subspaces are also adaptively adjusted according to the analyses of subspace optimization results of the previous iteration. Tests on 21 benchmark instances, comprising seven functions at 30, 60, and 90 dimensions, show that DA-EGO is effective on separable and partially separable problems under a budget of 1500 function evaluations. Its advantage is case-dependent: on the non-separable shifted Rosenbrock function, GSGA performs better at 60 and 90 dimensions, while the 30-dimensional results are statistically comparable to IKAEA and GSGA. Moreover, the advantage of DA-EGO is also seen in the aerodynamic optimization of a transonic rotor blade with 28 variables as well as the compressor stage optimization with 60 variables. With the above, the effectiveness of the proposed DA-EGO has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Semantics-Consistent Tabular Diffusion with Weak Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16069)
*Yili Wang,Ruxue Shi,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出\ours，一种语义一致的表格扩散框架，将弱指定的语义先验作为生成条件而非事后过滤，兼顾分布保真、语义一致与下游任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成器主要优化分布保真度，却忽略表格模式与文本描述中蕴含的弱语义先验，导致合成数据虽匹配真实分布，却可能违反语义约束（如有效行规则）。

Method: 先用LLM从元数据与描述中辅助抽取两类先验——列内语义与列间符号规则，并在真实训练集上验证；再将这些先验作为生成条件，把异构列值、列身份与语义先验映射到统一语义空间，通过列级前向加噪与先验条件反向去噪进行生成。

Result: 在六个真实表格基准上，\ours在分布保真度、语义一致性和下游任务效用方面均稳定优于VAE、GAN、LLM与扩散类代表基线；额外分析显示在先验部分缺失时也具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式建模并条件化弱语义先验，可有效弥合分布保真与语义有效之间的差距，提升合成表格数据的可用性。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data can match real data distributions while still violating the semantic constraints that govern valid tabular rows. This reveals a key limitation of existing tabular generators: they mainly optimize distributional fidelity, but do not explicitly model weak semantic priors encoded in tabular schema and textual descriptions. In this paper, we propose \ours, a semantics-consistent tabular diffusion framework for high-fidelity synthetic data generation under weakly specified semantic priors. \ours\ first constructs two types of priors, namely intra-column semantics and inter-column symbolic rules, with LLM-assisted extraction from metadata and validation on the real training split. These priors are then used as generation conditions rather than post-hoc filters. Specifically, \ours\ maps heterogeneous column values, column identities, and semantic priors into a unified semantic space, and performs column-wise forward corruption and prior-conditioned reverse denoising to preserve both marginal distributions and rule-consistent cross-column dependencies. Extensive experiments on six real-world tabular benchmarks show that \ours\ consistently improves distributional fidelity, semantic consistency, and downstream task utility over representative VAE-, GAN-, LLM-, and diffusion-based baselines. Additional analyses further demonstrate the robustness of \ours\ when semantic priors are partially unavailable.

</details>


### [106] [Schema-Adaptive Action-Conditioned JEPA for Cross-Machine CNC Transfer under Partial Sensor Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16071)
*Ayoub Louaye Bouaziz,Matthieu Ostertag,Anton Demasles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨机器部署下的工业世界模型迁移问题，提出SAAC-JEPA（模式自适应动作条件联合嵌入预测架构），用于数控机床动力学预测，并强调跨机器适配是独立于源域精度的评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业世界模型跨机器部署需应对动力学、传感接口、采样机制与控制单元的差异。源机有17个规范传感通道，目标机仅共享10个，存在部分传感器重叠，因此仅靠源域预测精度不足以评估工业预测表征的优劣。

Method: 采用模式自适应动作条件JEPA（SAAC-JEPA）建模数控动力学；评估采用组不交源域划分、仅源域归一化、留出自监督验证、单元审计及模型锁定后的封闭目标测试；通过仅源域20个候选搜索选择模式一致的动作条件JEPA，并做七种子稳定性检验；另设预声明的配对消融（同架构加入RevIN）与锁定前适配扫描。

Result: 五个种子下，JEPA预训练未带来干净源域预测增益（scratch与预训练体RMSE分别为0.811±0.022与0.813±0.022）。确认性目标测试中，锁定模型零样本RMSE=0.546、R²=0.012、NLL=0.52，优于持久性基线，但不及带RevIN的PatchTST与iTransformer基线（0.503与0.498）。同架构加入RevIN可将RMSE降至0.495±0.004，但使目标校准恶化（NLL=20.6）。锁定前适配扫描将RMSE进一步降至0.520，但目标支持有限。

Conclusion: 源域预测精度本身不足以评估工业预测表征；部分传感器重叠下的跨机器适配构成一个独立的评估轴，需结合目标域校准与稳定性综合考量。

Abstract: Cross-machine deployment of industrial world models requires transfer across changes in dynamics, sensing interfaces, sampling regimes, and control units. We study a schema-adaptive action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (SAAC-JEPA) for CNC dynamics, where the source machine has 17 canonical sensor channels and the target shares only 10. Evaluation uses group-disjoint source splits, source-only normalization, held-out self-supervised validation, unit audits, and a sealed target test after model locking. Across five seeds, JEPA pretraining gives no clean-source forecasting gain: scratch and pretrained-body models obtain \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.811\pm0.022\) and \(0.813\pm0.022\). A source-only search over 20 candidates selects a schema-consistent action-conditioned JEPA after seven-seed stability checks. On the confirmatory target pass, the locked model reaches zero-shot \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.546\), \(R^2=0.012\), and \(\mathrm{NLL}=0.52\), outperforming persistence but not RevIN-equipped PatchTST and iTransformer baselines (\(0.503\) and \(0.498\)). A pre-declared paired ablation shows that RevIN in the same architecture improves RMSE to \(0.495\pm0.004\) over three seeds, but degrades target calibration (\(\mathrm{NLL}=20.6\)) on stationary context windows. A pre-lock adaptation sweep further reduces RMSE to \(0.520\) with limited target support. These results show that source-domain forecasting accuracy alone is insufficient to assess industrial predictive representations, and that cross-machine adaptation under partial sensor overlap is a distinct evaluation axis.

</details>


### [107] [Pseudo-Label Augmentation for Affect Sensing in Small Collaborative Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16077)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Tanya Ignatenko,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Paolo Burelli,Jesper Bünsow Boldt,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在四人协作的自然场景情感感知中，标签稀疏是主要瓶颈。本文基于 GroupAffect-4 数据集，比较了无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权及人格+置信度联合加权四种策略，发现伪标签增强在已知团队设置下优于仅标注基线，但人格相似度范围过窄，主要起同队筛选作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然场景群体交互中的生理情感感知受限于标签稀疏而非传感器数据：可穿戴设备产生大量时间窗口，但自我报告每场仅采集几次。作者希望利用伪标签增强来缓解稀疏监督问题，并检验人格信息能否提供有效的信任加权。

Method: 使用 GroupAffect-4 四人协作数据集（含可穿戴生理信号、眼动追踪、大五人格及任务后 VAD 标签），在统一的目标构建流程下比较四种方案：无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权、人格+置信度联合加权。

Result: 伪标签增强在已知团队设置下优于仅标注基线。但大五人格余弦相似度范围狭窄（0.91–0.99），细粒度人格加权无效。平滑后，各增强 SVM 变体在效价和唤醒度上基本持平，人格+置信度联合变体在支配度上得分最高。跨被试 LOSO 迁移效果令人鼓舞（尤其唤醒度），而严格的会话隔离 LOGO 则消除了增强收益；鉴于仅 10 组，LOGO 应视为对未见团队迁移的保守下界。

Conclusion: 伪标签增强能更充分地利用稀疏标注的协作情感数据，而人格信息最有价值的用法是作为团队内筛选机制，而非校准的信任信号。

Abstract: Physiological affect sensing in naturalistic group interaction is often limited by sparse labels rather than sensor data: wearable devices produce many time windows, while self-reports are collected only a few times per session. Using GroupAffect-4, a four-person collaborative dataset with wearable physiology, eye tracking, Big Five personality, and post-task VAD labels, we study pseudo-label augmentation for affect sensing under sparse supervision.
